Potential issues:
- They update x via sign of gradient and normalize by max, causing very fast but plateau.
- Focus increases to 800 quickly, making gradient nearly only max lag. Could get stuck on single Lagrangian. Maybe better adapt p to active constraints with target flatness. Also they accept occasional weight decrease; but gradient could be dominated by many lags.
- In full optimizer, they do not exploit the fact objective scale independent; normalize every iteration. Good.
- The evaluation clipping min 1000, max 1000. For score, only sum matters; clipping 1000 non-issue if normalized.

Need propose better method. Ideas:

A. Use continuous optimization over a with projected gradient for smoothed max:
Objective f(a)=Σ_k exp(λ b_k - λ max b) gives smooth max. Gradient via FFT can be p_k = (exp(λ(b_k - maxb)))/(Σ exp?), as before. The optimal λ high. But high λ may cause instability. We can use an annealed λ and momentum/Nesterov, line search.

B. Use "smoothing of a" via total variation regularization? Not objective but helps escape? Maybe not.

C. Use dual iterative water-filling:
Given target T, we want find a≥0 sum=1 such that b_k≤T. This is feasibility. Use multiplicative updates: p_k = max(0, b_k - T) or exp((b-T)/epsilon). Update a_i ← a_i exp(-η Σ_k p_k a_{k-i}) (the subgradient). If infeasible, adjust T. But objective min max, we can directly minimize max by subgradient with active set. Binary local search:
Given x binary 0/1 (or near), a. We can compute current score. For each pair i, flipping x_i (swap a_i and a_{n-1-i}) changes a by Δ = t( e_i - e_r) if x_i 0->1 (reverse actually a_i +t, a_r -t). We want reduce max/new weighted sum.
Instead of evaluating all m flips with FFT each, compute effect vector Δb_i = t (b_shift(e_i) - b_shift(e_r))? Let's derive. b = a*a. If a' = a + Δ with Δ_i = δ e_i - δ e_r (δ=t if x from 0 to1; if from1to0 sign reversed). Δb = a*Δ + Δ*a + Δ*Δ. Since Δ sparse two points, a*Δ = δ a shifted by i - δ a shifted by r (in convolution index? δ e_i * a gives δ a at lags k=i+j; a*δ e_i = δ a at k=j+i same, so 2δ a shifted). Specifically for Δ = δ(e_i - e_r): Δb_k = 2δ [a_{k-i} - a_{k-r}] (valid) + δ^2[(1 if k=2i else -1? Wait Δ*Δ = δ^2(e_i*e_i - e_i*e_r - e_r*e_i + e_r*e_r) = δ^2( e_{2i} - e_{i+r} - e_{i+r}+e_{2r}) = δ^2(e_{2i}+e_{2r}-2e_{i+r}). But i+r=n-1. Since binary orientation swap within fixed pair? If a_i and a_r are t or0, δ=t. Δ*Δ fixed? If flipping from left to right: a_i 0->t, a_r t->0. Δb = 2t( a shifted i - a shifted r) + t^2(e_{2i}+e_{2r}-2e_{n-1}). Good independent of state. We can compute candidate max = max(current b + Δb). We could choose flip that minimizes weighted sum of top lags or approximate max.
Computing Δb for all i naively length 2n*m impossible. But we can compute for a chosen active lag k, the change Δb_k = 2t( a_{k-i}-a_{k-r}) + t^2(...). For top K lags, update those values for each flip in O(mK). K maybe 64. Use active list; choose best flip by weighted sum/estimated max. Repeat until no improvement or time. For each orientation vector s∈{0,1}^m, each pair p contributes to sums with all q. Can we get max count significantly below average m^2/(2n)=n/8? Some terms deterministic? For p=q: if p+q? For p=q, pos_p+pos_p can be 2p, p+r, or r+r. Orientation determines. For p+q=n-1: as noted center contributions when orientations equal. The probabilities: For generic p,q not related, left-left and right-right each prob1/4; cross prob1/4. For given k, there are multiple p,q pairs mapping to k. The terms are not independent because orientation p appears in m terms, causing variance ~m^3? We can approximate variance of C_k: indicator for pair (p,q) depends on s_p,s_q. Pair to a:
```
def _pair_to_a(x,n):
 m=n//2; t=1.0/m; a=np.empty(n); a[:m]=t*x; a[m:]=t*(1-x[::-1]); return a
```
For n even only. Could also handle odd: m=(n-1)//2, middle pair? For simplicity restrict n even. If given odd n, use full optimizer or pad to even? We can choose even lengths.

Projection of a to x:
```
def _project_pair_x(a,n):
 m=n//2; t=1.0/m; x=np.empty(m); s=a[:m]+a[m:][::-1]
 for i where s>eps: x[i]=a[i]/s * t? Wait a_i/(a_i+a_j) * t. If target pair sum t. x_i=(a_i)/(a_i+a_j) (since a_i'=t x_i, a_j'=t(1-x_i)); so x_i=a_i/s. Then a' pair sum=t. But total sum becomes m*t=1, independent of original sum? This rescales all pairs to fixed sum t; objective scale invariant, okay. If s=0, x=0.5.
```
This projection changes relative pair sums; given best might have pair sums not constant; projection might lose. But pair ansatz likely good. Could also not project but use full optimizer.

```
def _smooth_weights(b, focus, mode): # mode e.g. 'softmax'
 maxb=b.max()
 if maxb<=... uniform

```

`_fft_square(a)`:

```
L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(L); fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]; return np.maximum(b,0)
```

`_grad_pair(p,a)`:

def _binary_flip_optim(x0, time_limit, rng, n, K=64, verbose=False):

    while time:
       active_idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]  # or top union
       outside_max = np.partition(b, -K-1)[-K-1] if K+1 < len(b) else 0
       # compute Δ for active lags for all flips
       ks = active_idx.astype(np.int64)
       i_arr = np.arange(m)

       valsA=np.where(maskA, a[np.clip(idxA,0,n-1)],0.0)
       valsB=...
       diff = valsA - valsB

       quad = (ks==2*i_arr[:,None]) + (ks==2*r_arr[:,None]) - 2*(ks==n-1)
       new_active = b[ks][None,:] + delta

Subgradient optimizer (fast): reuse structure but with mixed weights. Let's code carefully.

Pseudo:
```
def _optimize_pair_subgrad(x0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label='', focus_start=10.0, focus_max=200.0, eta0=0.2, eta_min=0.01, eta_max=2.0):
    n=2*m; x=clip
    a=...
    b=None; score=inf
    best...
    focus=focus_start; focus_inc=1.01
    eta=eta0
    no_improve=0; max_no=800; resets=0; max_resets=30
    while time<limit:
       if b is None: b=_fft_square(a); score=...
       if score < best - ...: update
       else no_improve++
       if no_improve>max_no: reset (if resets>max_resets break)
          x=0.75*best + 0.25*rng.normal(0.5,0.3); clip
          focus = rng.choice([5,10,20,50,100,200])
          eta=0.2; b=None; continue
       p = _mixed_weights(b, focus, l2=0.2, uni=0.02)
       ga=_grad_pair(p,a); gx=ga[:m]-ga[m:][::-1]
       maxg = gx.max(abs)
       if maxg<1e-15: break
       x_new=clip(x - eta*(gx/maxg))
       if no change: x_new=clip(x+rng.normal(0,0.05))
       a_new=...; b_new=...; score_new=...
```

```
p=(1-l2-uni)*p_s + l2*p_l + uni*p_u
```

Binary polynomial:
We need update a/b exactly. Instead of recompute FFT after each accept, we can update b in O(n) using delta. Let's implement correctly to allow many flips.

Given current a length n, b length L. For flip at index i (left) and r=n-1-i:
Let δ = +t if x_i currently 0 (a_i=0,a_r=t) flipping to 1 (a_i=t,a_r=0), so δ = +t if a_i < a_r else -t. More generally if x_i not exactly binary, flip continuous? We'll restrict binary: x_i =0 -> δ=+t; x_i=1 -> δ=-t. The new a = a + δ e_i - δ e_r.
The change in linear convolution b_new[k] = b[k] + δ*(a shifted i twice? Let's derive:
b(a+Δ) = b(a) + b(a,Δ)+b(Δ,a)+b(Δ,Δ).
Since b is linear convolution not correlation, for sparse Δ=δ(e_i - e_r):
b(a,Δ)[k] = Σ_j a_j Δ_{k-j} = δ(a_{k-i} - a_{k-r}) if indices valid.
b(Δ,a)[k] = Σ_j Δ_j a_{k-j} = δ(a_{k-i} - a_{k-r})? Wait if Δ_j=δ e_{j-i} - δ e_{j-r}; term b(Δ,a)_k = δ a_{k-i} - δ a_{k-r}. Same. So total linear 2δ(a_{k-i}-a_{k-r}) with valid.
b(Δ,Δ): δ^2 [e_{2i} - e_{i+r} - e_{r+i} + e_{2r}] = δ^2(e_{2i}+e_{2r}-2e_{n-1}).
Yes.
So to update b for accepted flip:
```
def _add_flip_delta(b, a, j, δ):
    i=j; r=n-1-j
    # linear part: create temp? For k range max(0,i) to min(L-1,i+n-1): b[k]+=2δ*a[k-i]
    # for r similarly negative
    start_i=max(0,i); end_i=min(L, i+n)  # k-i in [0,n-1]
    b[start_i:end_i] += 2*δ*a[start_i-i:end_i-i]
    start_r=max(0,r); end_r=min(L, r+n)
    b[start_r:end_r] -= 2*δ*a[start_r-r:end_r-r]
    # quad: if 2i<L: b[2i]+=δ^2; if 2r<L: b[2r]+=δ^2; b[n-1]-=2δ^2
```

Implementation of O(n) update:
```
def _apply_flip_delta(b, a_old, j, delta, n,t):
   L=len(b)
   i=j; r=n-1-j
   si=max(0,i); ei=min(L,i+n)
   if si<ei: b[si:ei] += 2*delta*a_old[si-i:ei-i]
   sr=max(0,r); er=min(L,r+n)
   if sr<er: b[sr:er] -= 2*delta*a_old[sr-r:er-r]
   qi=2*i
   if 0<=qi<L: b[qi] += delta*delta
   qr=2*r
   if 0<=qr<L: b[qr] += delta*delta
   b[n-1] -= 2*delta*delta
```

`_fft_square(a)`:
```
n=int(a.size)
L=2*n-1
N=_pow2(L)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=N).real[:L]
np.maximum(b,0.0,out=b)
return b
```

`_score_from_arrays(a,b)`:

```
s=float(a.sum())
if s<=1e-300: return inf
return float(2.0*a.size*float(b.max())/(s*s))
```
We usually normalize sum=1; use exact.

```
def _project_x_from_a(a,n):
   m=n//2
   s=a[:m]+a[m:][::-1]
   x=np.full(m,0.5)
   mask=s>1e-12
   x[mask]=a[:m][mask]/s[mask]
   # if n odd? skip
   return np.clip(x,1e-8,1-1e-8)
```

`_upsample_pair(x, m_new)`:
```
x=np.asarray(x); m_old=x.size
if m_old==1: return np.full(m_new, x[0])
pos_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m_old,endpoint=False)
pos_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m_new,endpoint=False)
x_new=np.interp(pos_new, pos_old, x, period=1.0)
return np.clip(x_new,1e-8,1-1e-8)
```
If old from nonperiodic, period helps.

Code from previous:
```
def _greedy_x(n, rng, shuffle=True):
    m=n//2
    counts=np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int64)
    selected=np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
    order=np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if shuffle: rng.shuffle(order)
    t=0
    for idx_val in order:
       idx=int(idx_val); l=idx; r=n-1-idx
       if t==0: y=l
       else:
          s=selected[:t]
          affected_l=int(np.max(counts[s+l]))
          self_l=int(counts[2*l]); metric_l=max(affected_l+2,self_l+1)
          affected_r=int(np.max(counts[s+r])); self_r=int(counts[2*r]); metric_r=max(affected_r+2,self_r+1)
          y = l if metric_l <= metric_r else r
       if t>0: counts[selected[:t]+y] += 2
       counts[2*y] += 1
       selected[t]=y; t+=1
    x=np.full(m,1e-6)
    x[sel < m]=1.0-1e-6
    return x
```
But `selected[:t] + y` creates temp each step; okay. Could optimize with cumulative but fine.
Actually counts selected representation: selected elements are actual indices (left or right). The constraint: we select exactly one from each pair index idx; selected list holds actual positions. The metric for choosing y uses existing selected. It adds 2 for each pair with existing plus self 1? Since flipping orientation adds an element; self convolution δ^2 gives one at 2y; cross with each existing contributes 2. Good. The initial t==0 choose l; could random. Good.
Potential issue: `counts[2*r]` for r>=m, 2r may exceed 2n-2 (L-1). np max index? `counts[2*r]` if 2*r >= L would IndexError! For r=n-1-m? Max r=n-1, 2r=2n-2 = L-1 okay. For r up to n-1, 2r <=2n-2. Good. For l max m-1, 2l <=2m-2 <L.
`counts[s+l]`: s actual index up to n-1, l up to m-1, sum up to 2n-2 okay.

But greedy selected pairs out of order; if it chooses right for a later idx, selected includes right index. The pair idx not yet selected unaffected. Good.

This produces x for first-half pair variables? x[j]=1 if selected[j] (actual position) is left? Wait selected[t]=y where y is actual index if pair idx_val selected? It can be left l (idx) or right r (n-1-idx). x is indexed by pair idx 0..m-1. If selected[t] < m (left), x[paired idx]=1; if selected[t] >=m (right), x[paired idx]=0? But when y is right for pair idx_val, selected[t]=r. In code final `left = sel < m; x[sel[left]]=1` would incorrectly index by actual left positions, not pair idx. The previous code: `y = l if ...`, selected[t]=y; later `left = sel < m; x[sel[left]]=1.0 -1e-6`. If y=l (left) for pair idx, x[idx]=1. If y=r (right), sel is right index, sel< m false, so x remains 0 for that pair. The pair index for right r is n-1-r = idx. But x index should idx, not r. The previous code uses x[sel[left]]=1, and for right leaves x corresponding to its left pair index? Since x initially 0, yes x[idx] remains 0; but how know idx? For right positions selected, no x set, okay because pair's left default 0. But if right position r coincides with some left index? r>=m, so sel[left] only left. Thus x indices set for left choices; right choices default 0. That works because each pair default 0 (right). But what if pair index idx left position equals an earlier right position? No, left indices 0..m-1 disjoint from right m..n-1. So setting x[actual left position]=1 corresponds to pair idx left. Good.
Tie: if y is left for pair idx, x[idx]=1. If y is right, x[idx]=0. Good.

Now `_mixed_weights`:
```
def _mixed_weights(b, focus, l2_weight=0.25, uniform_weight=0.02, power=2.0):
    L=int(b.size)
    maxb=float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L,1.0/L)
    z=((maxb-b)/maxb)
    with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore'):
        ps=np.exp(-float(focus)*z)
    ps=np.maximum(ps, 0.0)
    s=ps.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
       ps=np.zeros(L); ps[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
    else:
       ps=ps/s
    # l2 weight based on b^power
    base=np.maximum(b,0.0)/maxb
    if power !=1: pl=base**power
    else: pl=base
    sl=pl.sum()
    if sl <=1e-12: pl=ps.copy()
    else: pl=pl/sl
    p=(1.0-l2_weight-uniform_weight)*ps + l2_weight*pl + uniform_weight*(1.0/L)
    sp=p.sum()
    if sp<=0: p=np.full(L,1/L)
    else: p=p/sp
    return p
```
If l2+uniform >1? choose <1. We'll pass l2=0.25, uniform=0.02.

`_fft_grad(p,a)` as previous. Need N = _pow2(len(p)+a.size-1). But if len(p)=2n-1, a=n, N maybe 4n? For n=8192, L=16383, L+n-1=24575, pow2=32768. Rfft length 32768. Good. If use same N for square? square L=16383, pow2=16384. Different.
```
def _fft_grad(weights,a):
    n=int(a.size); L=int(weights.size); N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(weights,n=N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa),n=N)[:n]
    return 2.0*c
```
Potential performance: For each iteration, p changes, a changes; okay.

`_gradient_pair(p,a,n)`:
```
g=_fft_grad(p,a); m=n//2; return g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]
```
Could avoid full g length n by computing only? no.

`_score_from_b` uses a.sum; for pair, sum 1. For candidate from `_pair_to_a`, sum may be 1; for full, normalize.

Subgradient pair optimizer:
Need manage time robustly. Let's code:
```
def _optimize_pair_subgrad(x0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="", focus_start=10.0, focus_max=300.0, eta0=0.25):
    x=np.asarray(x0,dtype=float).copy(); m=x.size; n=2*m
    x=np.clip(x,1e-8,1-1e-8)
    a=_pair_to_a(x,n); b=None; score=inf
    best_x=x.copy(); best_score=inf
    focus=focus_start; focus_inc=1.012
    eta=eta0; eta_min=0.01; eta_max=3.0
    no_imp=0; max_no=800; resets=0; max_resets=30
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if b is None:
          b=_fft_square(a); score=_score_from_arrays(a,b)
       if score < best_score - 1e-10:
          best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); no_imp=0
       else:
          no_imp +=1
       if verbose and it%2000==0: print...
       if no_imp > max_no:
          resets+=1
          if resets>max_resets: break
          no_imp=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.25)
          if rng.random()<0.5:
             x=0.75*best_x + 0.25*rng.normal(0.5,0.3,size=m)
          else:
             x=best_x + rng.normal(0.0,0.15,size=m)
          x=np.clip(x,1e-8,1-1e-8)
          a=_pair_to_a(x,n); b=None; score=inf
          focus=float(rng.choice([5.0,10.0,20.0,50.0,100.0,200.0]))
          eta=0.25
          continue
       p=_mixed_weights(b, focus, 0.25, 0.02)
       gx=_gradient_pair(p,a,n)
       maxg=float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
       if maxg < 1e-18: break
       x_new=np.clip(x - eta*(gx/maxg), 1e-8,1-1e-8)
       if not np.any(x_new != x):
          x_new=np.clip(x + rng.normal(0,0.05,m),1e-8,1-1e-8)
       a_new=_pair_to_a(x_new,n); b_new=_fft_square(a_new); score_new=_score_from_arrays(a_new,b_new)
       wt_old=float(np.dot(p,b)); wt_new=float(np.dot(p,b_new))
       accept = False
       if score_new < best_score - 1e-10: accept=True
       elif score_new < score - 1e-12: accept=True
       elif score_new < score*1.003 and wt_new < wt_old*0.999: accept=True
       if accept:
          x=x_new; a=a_new; b=b_new; score=score_new
          eta=min(eta*1.05, eta_max)
       else:
          eta=max(eta*0.75, eta_min)
          if eta < 0.04:
             x=np.clip(x+rng.normal(0,0.05,m),...)
             a=_pair_to_a(x,n); b=None; score=inf
       focus=min(focus*focus_inc, focus_max)
       it+=1
    # ensure best_x recompute? return
    best_a=_pair_to_a(best_x,n)
    return best_a, best_score
```
One issue: If `b` is None after reset, at top update best? It recomputes before update. Good. `score` for reset inf; after recompute, no_improve maybe large but reset sets no_imp=0. Good.
Accept surrogate: `wt_new < wt_old*0.999` maybe too strict for small values. Use `wt_new < wt_old - 1e-12` or relative 1e-6. We'll use `wt_new < wt_old * (1.0 - 1e-6)`.

Adam pair optimizer:
```
def _optimize_pair_adam(x0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="", focus_start=20, focus_max=400, lr0=0.03):
    x... a... b...
    m1=np.zeros(m); v1=np.zeros(m); step=0
    beta1=0.7; beta2=0.9; eps=1e-8
    lr=lr0; lr_min=0.002; lr_max=0.25
    focus=focus_start; focus_inc=1.006
    no_imp=0; max_no=600; resets=0; max_resets=25
    while time<limit:
       if b None compute
       if score<best: update
       else no_imp++
       if no_imp>max_no: reset similar but focus random, lr=lr0, moments reset, x blend; continue
       p=_mixed_weights(b, focus, 0.25, 0.02)
       gx=_gradient_pair(p,a,n)
       maxg=max(abs)
       if maxg<1e-18: break
       gd=gx/maxg
       step+=1
       m1=beta1*m1+(1-beta1)*gd
       v1=beta2*v1+(1-beta2)*gd*gd
       mhat=m1/(1-beta1**step)
       vhat=v1/(1-beta2**step)
       update=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       x_try=np.clip(x - lr*update,1e-8,1-1e-8)
       if np.count_nonzero(x_try != x) ==0: x_try=clip(x+rng.normal(0,0.03,m),...)
       a_try... score_try
       wt_old,wt_new
       accept if score_try < best-1e-10 or < score-1e-12 or (< score*1.003 and wt_new < wt_old*(1-1e-6))
       if accept:
          x=x_try; a=a_try; b=b_try; score=score_try
          lr=min(lr*1.03,lr_max)
       else:
          lr=max(lr*0.8,lr_min)
          if lr < 0.005:
             x=clip(x+rng.normal(0,0.05,m))
             moments=0; step=0; a=...; b=None
       focus=min(focus*focus_inc, focus_max)
       # maybe if no_imp large, increase focus by factor? focus already increases.
    return best_x,_optimize? best_a,score
```

```
def _optimize_full_subgrad(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label=""):
    a=np.asarray... clean, normalize to sum1, max 1e-15
    b=None; score=inf; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=inf

    no_imp=0; max_no=800; resets=0; max_resets=25
    while time:
      if b None b=_fft_square(a); score=...
      update best/no_imp
      if reset: if rng<0.5: x0=random_pair_x if n even else random; a_mix=0.7*best_a+0.3*pair_a; else a=best_a*exp(normal); normalize; b=None; focus random; eta=0.25
      p=_mixed_weights(b,focus,0.25,0.02)
      g=_fft_grad(p,a)
      mean=float(np.dot(a,g)); # since sum=1
      gd=g-mean
      maxg=max(abs(gd)); if < break
      a_try=a*np.exp(-eta*gd/maxg)
      a_try=np.maximum(a_try,1e-300); s=a_try.sum(); a_try/=s
      b_try=_fft_square(a_try); score_try=...
      wt_old=dot(p,b); wt_new=...
      accept...
      focus...
    return best_a,best_score
```

Binary polish:
Implement but ensure no too long. Let's code carefully.
```
def _pair_binary_polish(x0, time_limit, rng, n, K=48, subset=256, verbose=False):
    x=(x0>0.5).astype(np.float64)
    # if all same? add random? if np.all(x==0.5) etc
    a=_pair_to_a(x,n); b=_fft_square(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy(); best_x=x.copy(); maxb=float(b.max()); best_score=2*n*maxb
    m=x.size; t=1.0/m; L=2*n-1
    no_imp=0; max_no=2000; it=0; recomp=0
    t0=...
    while time<limit:
       # estimate outside max
       if K < L-1:
           outside_max = float(np.partition(b, L-K-1)[L-K-1])
       else: outside_max=0.0
       # active indices top K
       active = np.argpartition(b, L-K)[-K:] if K < L else np.arange(L)
       S = min(subset, m)
       idx = rng.choice(m, size=S, replace=False) if S < m else np.arange(m)
       ii = idx.astype(np.int64)[:,None]
       rr = n-1 - ii
       ks = active.astype(np.int64)[None,:]
       x_sub = x[idx]
       delta = t*(1.0 - 2.0*x_sub)[:,None]
       idx1 = ks - ii
       idx2 = ks - rr
       mask1 = (idx1>=0)&(idx1<n); mask2=(idx2>=0)&(idx2<n)
       a1 = np.where(mask1, a[np.clip(idx1,0,n-1)], 0.0)
       a2 = np.where(mask2, a[np.clip(idx2,0,n-1)], 0.0)
       q = np.zeros_like(delta)
       q += (ks == 2*ii).astype(float)
       q += (ks == 2*rr).astype(float)
       q -= 2.0*(ks == n-1).astype(float)
       db = 2.0*delta*(a1-a2) + (t*t)*q
       new_active = b[ks] + db
       est_max = np.maximum(outside_max, np.max(new_active, axis=1))
       j_local = int(np.argmin(est_max))
       j = int(idx[j_local])
       old_max = maxb
       # If estimate not better and surrogate not better? We still may apply if est barely lower? Use if est_max[j] < old_max - 1e-12 else random flip if no_improve? 
       if est_max[j_local] < old_max - 1e-14 or no_imp > 1000:
           old_x = float(x[j]); old_ai=float(a[j]); old_ar=float(a[n-1-j])
           b_old=b.copy()
           d = t*(1.0 - 2.0*old_x)
           # apply delta to b using current a old
           _apply_flip_delta(b, a, j, n, d, L)
           # update a/x
           x[j] = 1.0 - old_x
           a[j] = old_ai + d
           a[n-1-j] = old_ar - d
           new_max = float(b.max())
           if new_max < old_max - 1e-15:
               maxb = new_max
               best_score=2*n*new_max
               best_a=a.copy(); best_b=b.copy(); best_x=x.copy(); no_imp=0
               # recompute periodically
               if (it % 100)==0? It increments each pass? Let's recompute every 50 accepted:
               accepted_count += 1; if accepted_count % 50 ==0: b=_fft_square(a); maxb=float(b.max())
           else:
               # revert
               b = b_old
               x[j]=old_x; a[j]=old_ai; a[n-1-j]=old_ar
               no_imp +=1
       else:
           no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > max_no:
           # reset with random flips or restart; maybe break/rebuild
           # Perform random batch flips to escape
           for _ in range(min(50,m)): flip random? or x = 0.5 +0.5*randn? 
           x = 0.5 + 0.5*rng.normal(0,1,m); x=np.clip(x,0,1); x=(x>0.5).astype(float)
           a=_pair_to_a(x,n); b=_fft_square(a); maxb=float(b.max()); reset no_imp; if maxb>best_score/(2n)*1.1 maybe break
       it +=1
    return best_x, best_score
```
Need `_apply_flip_delta` top-level with `L` maybe.
```
def _apply_flip_delta(b, a, j, n, delta, L):
    i=j; r=n-1-j; d2=delta*delta
    si=max(0,i); ei=min(L,i+n)
    if si<ei: b[si:ei] += 2.0*delta*a[si-i:ei-i]
    sr=max(0,r); er=min(L,r+n)
    if sr<er: b[sr:er] -= 2.0*delta*a[sr-r:er-r]
    qi=2*i
    if 0<=qi<L: b[qi] += d2
    qr=2*r
    if 0<=qr<L: b[qr] += d2
    b[n-1] -= 2.0*d2
```
If `b[n-1]` also in windows, we already added linear to b[n-1]; then subtract 2d2. Good.
Rounding could produce negative; after recompute, ok. If new_max uses b.max and small negative ignored.

But if we revert by `b=b_old`, active? okay. Need `old_ai+delta` may be negative? For binary, old_ai either 0 or t; delta ±t; new 0/t. Good. If x not exactly binary due previous? We set x binary at start. Good.

```
    for f in freqs:
      for beta in betas:

           x=_sine_pair_x(m,f,beta,phase)
           sc=_score_pair_x(x,n)
           if sc<best_s: ...
    return best_x
```

Let's verify derivative formula with a simple n=2 case to ensure sign. n=2, a=[a0,a1], b=[a0^2,2a0a1,a1^2]. p weights length3. Derivative p·b wrt a0 = 2 a0 p0 + 2 a1 p1. Our _fft_grad: c_i=Σ_k p_k a_{k-i}. For i=0: k=0..2, a_{k} => p0 a0 + p1 a1 + p2 a2(0)=p0 a0+p1 a1. *2 => 2p0a0+2p1a1. Good. i=1: k-1 in 0..1 => k=1 p1 a0 + k=2 p2 a1 => 2p1a0+2p2a1. Correct. Good.

For pair derivative: n=2, m=1, x: a0=t x, a1=t(1-x). b center=2a0a1. derivative wrt x = t(∂/∂a0 - ∂/∂a1)=t(2p1 a1 - 2p1 a0)=2t p1(a1-a0). Our gx = g0-g1 = 2p1a1 - 2p1a0. good.

`_consider_pair_x`:
```
def _consider_pair_x(x,n,best_a,best_score,best_x,best_n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n//2 or n%2 !=0: return best_a,best_score,best_x,best_n
    a=_pair_to_a(x,n)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <=0.01: return ...
    b=_fft_square(a)
    sc=float(2.0*n*float(b.max())/(s*s))
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
       x_clip=np.clip(x,0,1).copy()
       return a.copy(), float(sc), x_clip, int(n)
    return best_a,best_score,best_x,best_n
```

```
       new_x=None

    return...
```

Let's define `_consider_pair_x_update` exactly:
```
def _consider_pair_x_update(x, n, overall_a, overall_score, pair_x, pair_n, pair_a, pair_score):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n is None or n <= 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return overall_a, overall_score, pair_x, pair_n, pair_a, pair_score
    m = n // 2
    if x.size != m:
        x = _upsample_pair(x, m)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return ...
    b = _fft_square(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (s * s))
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if sc < overall_score:
        overall_a = a.copy(); overall_score = sc
    if sc < pair_score:
        pair_x = x.copy(); pair_n = int(n); pair_a = a.copy(); pair_score = sc
    return overall_a, overall_score, pair_x, pair_n, pair_a, pair_score
```
This recomputes b; tasks will recompute in optimizer anyway.

`_consider_full_update`:
```
def _consider_full_update(a, overall_a, overall_score):
    a=_clean_array(a)
    if a.size<2: return
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0.01 return
    a=a/s
    b=_fft_square(a); sc=...
    if finite and sc<overall_score: return a.copy(),sc
    return overall_a, overall_score
```

`_project_pair_from_a` for odd? returns None:
```
def _project_pair_from_a(a,n):
   if n is None: n=a.size
   if n%2!=0 or n<2: return None
   m=n//2
   s=a[:m]+a[m:][::-1]
   x=np.full(m,0.5)
   mask=s>1e-12
   x[mask]=a[:m][mask]/s[mask]
   return np.clip(x,1e-8,1-1e-8)
```

`_clean_array` used in full. For pair x, clean.

Let's write `_pair_binary_polish` more carefully.
```
def _pair_binary_polish(x0, time_limit, rng, n, K=96, subset=256):
    m=n//2
    x=np.asarray(x0).ravel().copy()
    x=(x>0.5).astype(np.float64)
    # If all same or too uniform? x = random binary if no variation? It will have variation.
    a=_pair_to_a(x,n)
    b=_fft_square(a)
    maxb=float(b.max())
    best_x=x.copy(); best_score=float(2.0*n*maxb)
    t=1.0/m; L=2*n-1
    no_imp=0; max_no=5000; accepted=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if K >= L-1:
           outside_max=0.0
           active=np.arange(L)
           Keff=L
       else:
           Keff=K
           active=np.argpartition(b, L-Keff)[-Keff:]
           outside_max=float(np.partition(b, L-Keff-1)[L-Keff-1])
       S=min(subset,m)
       if S == m:
           idx=np.arange(m)
       else:
           idx=rng.choice(m, size=S, replace=False)
       ii=idx.astype(np.int64)[:,None]
       rr=n-1 - ii
       ks=active.astype(np.int64)[None,:]
       delta=t*(1.0 - 2.0*x[idx])[:,None]
       idx1=ks - ii
       idx2=ks - rr
       mask1=(idx1>=0)&(idx1<n); mask2=(idx2>=0)&(idx2<n)
       a1=np.where(mask1, a[np.clip(idx1,0,n-1)],0.0)
       a2=np.where(mask2, a[np.clip(idx2,0,n-1)],0.0)
       q=(ks == 2*ii).astype(np.float64) + (ks == 2*rr).astype(np.float64) - 2.0*(ks == n-1).astype(np.float64)
       db=2.0*delta*(a1-a2) + (t*t)*q
       new_active=b[ks][None,:] + db
       # Use current outside_max; if active contains max, outside < max. Estimate:
       est_max=np.maximum(outside_max, np.max(new_active, axis=1))
       j_local=int(np.argmin(est_max))
       j=int(idx[j_local])
       if est_max[j_local] < maxb - 1e-15 or no_imp > 800:
           old_x=float(x[j]); old_ai=float(a[j]); old_ar=float(a[n-1-j])
           b_old=b.copy()
           d=t*(1.0-2.0*old_x)
           _apply_flip_delta(b, a, j, n, d, L)
           x[j]=1.0-old_x; a[j]=old_ai+d; a[n-1-j]=old_ar-d
           new_max=float(b.max())
           if new_max < maxb - 1e-15:
               maxb=new_max
               best_score=2.0*n*new_max
               best_x=x.copy()
               no_imp=0
               accepted +=1
               if accepted % 100 == 0 or accepted > 1000:
                   b=_fft_square(a)
                   maxb=float(b.max())
           else:
               b=b_old
               x[j]=old_x; a[j]=old_ai; a[n-1-j]=old_ar
               no_imp +=1
       else:
           no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > max_no:
           # random restart local
           x=(x>0.5); # current
           if rng.random()<0.5:
               x=0.5 + 0.5*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,m)
               x=(x>0.5).astype(np.float64)
           else:
               # flip random 10%
               flip_idx=rng.choice(m, size=min(m, max(1,m//10)), replace=False)
               x[flip_idx]=1.0-x[flip_idx]
           a=_pair_to_a(x,n)
           b=_fft_square(a)
           maxb=float(b.max())
           # if too far from best, maybe continue to next; but reset no_imp
           no_imp=0
           max_no=int(max_no*1.2)
           if maxb > best_score/(2.0*n)*1.5:
               # if reset worse than best by a lot, maybe continue, but keep best
               pass
       it +=1
    return best_x, best_score
```

```
def _run_pair_kind(kind, n, x, time_limit, rng, label=""):
    x_arr=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float).ravel(),0,1)
    if time_limit <= 20:
       a=_pair_to_a(x_arr,n); b=_fft_square(a); sc=_score_from_arrays(a,b); return a,sc,x_arr
    if kind == 'subgrad':
       a,sc,x_out=_optimize_pair_subgrad(x_arr,time_limit,rng,label=label,verbose=False)
       return a,sc,x_out
    if kind == 'adam': ...
    if kind == 'binary':
       x_out,sc=_pair_binary_polish(x_arr,time_limit,rng,n)
       a=_pair_to_a(x_out,n); return a,sc,x_out
    # default subgrad
```

Now, final answer code. Let's produce.
</think>

```python
"""
Portfolio search for nonnegative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Main ideas:
- Use the anti-palindromic pair ansatz a[i] + a[n-1-i] = 2/n,
  parameterized by x[i] in [0, 1].  This removes one null direction and is
  effective for pushing down the central convolution coefficient.
- Optimize x with:
  * smoothed-max subgradient (softmax weights on large convolution terms),
  * Adam-style smoothed-max optimization,
  * a stochastic binary flip polish on the pair variables.
- Use several starting points: projection of the given best, greedy binary
  anti-palindromic supports, Thue-Morse-like signs, sinusoidal/Four seeds,
  random seeds, and upsamples of previous bests.
- Finally, polish the best pair candidate; if the best overall candidate is
  not on the pair manifold, also polish it with a full sequence optimizer.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _now():
    return time.monotonic()


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def _fft_square(a):
    """Full linear convolution a * a, using FFT."""
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def _score_from_arrays(a, b):
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(b.max()) / (s * s))


def _pair_to_a(x, n):
    """
    Convert pair variables x of length m=n/2 into a length-n sequence:
        a[i] = x[i]/m, a[n-1-i] = (1-x[i])/m.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(x.size)
    if n is None or int(n) != 2 * m or m <= 0:
        n = 2 * m
    n = int(n)
    t = 1.0 / m
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = t * x
    a[m:] = t * (1.0 - x[::-1])
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return a


def _score_pair_x(x, n):
    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    b = _fft_square(a)
    return _score_from_arrays(a, b)


def _project_pair_from_a(a, n=None):
    """Project an even-length nonnegative sequence onto the pair manifold."""
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n is None:
        n = int(a.size)
    else:
        n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or n % 2 != 0 or a.size < n:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    s = a[:m] + a[m:][::-1]
    x = np.full(m, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = s > 1e-12
    if np.any(mask):
        x[mask] = a[:m][mask] / s[mask]
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _upsample_pair(x, m_new):
    """Periodic linear interpolation of pair variables to a new length."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m_old = int(x.size)
    m_new = int(m_new)
    if m_old <= 0 or m_new <= 0:
        return np.full(m_new, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    if m_old == 1:
        return np.full(m_new, float(x[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    pos_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_old, endpoint=False)
    pos_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_new, endpoint=False)
    y = np.interp(pos_new, pos_old, x, period=1.0)
    return np.clip(y, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _random_pair_x(m, rng):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 0.5 + 0.45 * (rng.random(m) - 0.5) * 2.0
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _random_binary_x(m, rng):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (rng.random(m) < 0.5).astype(np.float64)
    # Push slightly off exact 0/1 for checkpoint safety.
    x[x <= 0.5] = 1e-8
    x[x > 0.5] = 1.0 - 1e-8
    return x


def _thue_pair_x(m, rng):
    """Anti-palindromic-like Thue-Morse sign pattern turned into [0,1]."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    while z.size < m:
        z = np.concatenate((z, -z))
    z = z[:m]
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        z = -z
    amp = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.35, 0.55, 0.75, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)))
    x = 0.5 + 0.5 * amp * z
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _sine_pair_x(m, f, beta, phase):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x = 0.5 + 0.5 * float(beta) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * i / float(m) + float(phase))
    return np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)


def _good_sine_seed(n, rng):
    """Quick low-dimensional Fourier seed search for the pair ansatz."""
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    best_x = _sine_pair_x(m, 1.0, 1.0, 0.0)
    best_s = _score_pair_x(best_x, n)

    freqs = [0.5, 1.0, 1.3, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0]
    betas = [0.5, 0.8, 1.0]
    phases = [0.0, 0.9, 2.0, float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))]

    for f in freqs:
        for beta in betas:
            for ph in phases:
                x = _sine_pair_x(m, f, beta, ph)
                s = _score_pair_x(x, n)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                    best_s = s
                    best_x = x

    # A few random two-mode seeds.
    for _ in range(18):
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 9.0))
        f2 = f1 * float(rng.uniform(1.15, 3.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(0.45, 1.0))
        i = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
        v = w1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * i / m + p1) + w2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * i / m + p2)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(v))) if v.size else 1.0
        if scale < 1e-12:
            continue
        x = 0.5 + 0.5 * beta * (v / scale)
        x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        s = _score_pair_x(x, n)
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best_x = x

    return best_x


def _greedy_x(n, rng, shuffle=True):
    """
    Greedy binary anti-palindromic support.  For each symmetric pair
    choose the side that minimizes a proxy for future collision counts.
    """
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)
    selected = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
    order = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    if shuffle:
        rng.shuffle(order)

    t = 0
    for idx_val in order:
        idx = int(idx_val)
        l = idx
        r = n - 1 - idx

        if t == 0:
            y = l
        else:
            ssel = selected[:t]
            affected_l = int(np.max(counts[ssel + l]))
            self_l = int(counts[2 * l])
            metric_l = max(affected_l + 2, self_l + 1)

            affected_r = int(np.max(counts[ssel + r]))
            self_r = int(counts[2 * r])
            metric_r = max(affected_r + 2, self_r + 1)

            y = l if metric_l <= metric_r else r

        if t > 0:
            counts[selected[:t] + y] += 2
        counts[2 * y] += 1
        selected[t] = y
        t += 1

    x = np.full(m, 1e-8, dtype=np.float64)
    left = selected < m
    if np.any(left):
        x[selected[left]] = 1.0 - 1e-8
    return x


def _mixed_weights(b, focus, l2_weight=0.25, uniform_weight=0.02, power=2.0):
    """
    Weight distribution used as a smoothed max subgradient:
    mostly softmax on large b, plus an L2-like high-b weight and uniform bias.
    """
    L = int(b.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    z = (maxb - b) / maxb
    with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore"):
        ps = np.exp(-float(focus) * z)
    ps = np.maximum(ps, 0.0)
    s = float(ps.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        ps = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        ps[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
    else:
        ps /= s

    base = np.maximum(b, 0.0) / maxb
    if power != 1.0:
        pl = np.power(base, float(power))
    else:
        pl = base.copy()
    sl = float(pl.sum())
    if sl <= 1e-12:
        pl = ps.copy()
    else:
        pl /= sl

    p = (1.0 - l2_weight - uniform_weight) * ps + l2_weight * pl + uniform_weight * (1.0 / L)
    sp = float(p.sum())
    if sp <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p /= sp
    return p


def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    """
    Gradient of p^T(a*a) with respect to a, where p is weights of length 2n-1.
    For fixed p, derivative is 2 * cross-correlation(p, a) sampled at 0..n-1.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(weights.size)
    N = _pow2(L + n - 1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(weights, n=N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N)[:n]
    return 2.0 * c


def _pair_gradient(p, a, n):
    """Gradient with respect to pair variables x."""
    g = _fft_grad(p, a)
    m = n // 2
    return g[:m] - g[m:][::-1]


def _optimize_pair_subgrad(x0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="",
                           focus_start=10.0, focus_max=300.0, eta0=0.25):
    """
    Smoothed-max subgradient ascent on pair variables x.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    m = int(x.size)
    n = 2 * m
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf"), x

    x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    b = None
    score = float("inf")

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = float("inf")

    focus = float(focus_start)
    focus_inc = 1.012
    eta = float(eta0)
    eta_min = 0.01
    eta_max = 3.0

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 900
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 30

    t0 = _now()
    it = 0

    while _now() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score = _score_from_arrays(a, b)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_score = score
            best_x = x.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0):
            print(f"[{label}] subgrad it={it} t={_now()-t0:.1f}s score={score:.6f} "
                  f"best={best_score:.6f} focus={focus:.2f} eta={eta:.3f} resets={resets}")

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.25)
            if rng.random() < 0.55:
                x = 0.75 * best_x + 0.25 * rng.normal(0.5, 0.30, size=m)
            else:
                x = best_x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, size=m)
            x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
            a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            focus = float(rng.choice(np.array([5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0, 160.0, 300.0])))
            eta = 0.25
            continue

        p = _mixed_weights(b, focus, 0.25, 0.02)
        gx = _pair_gradient(p, a, n)
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
        if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg < 1e-18:
            break

        x_new = x - eta * (gx / maxg)
        x_new = np.nan_to_num(x_new, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        x_new = np.clip(x_new, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

        if not np.any(x_new != x):
            x_new = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

        a_new = _pair_to_a(x_new, n)
        b_new = _fft_square(a_new)
        score_new = _score_from_arrays(a_new, b_new)

        wt_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        wt_new = float(np.dot(p, b_new))

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(score_new):
            if score_new < best_score - 1e-10:
                accept = True
            elif score_new < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif score_new < score * 1.003 and wt_new < wt_old * (1.0 - 1e-6):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            x = x_new
            a = a_new
            b = b_new
            score = score_new
            eta = min(eta * 1.05, eta_max)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.75, eta_min)
            if eta < 0.04:
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
                x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
                a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
                b = None
                score = float("inf")

        focus = min(focus * focus_inc, float(focus_max))
        it += 1

    best_a = _pair_to_a(best_x, n)
    return best_a, float(best_score), best_x


def _optimize_pair_adam(x0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="",
                        focus_start=30.0, focus_max=450.0, lr0=0.03):
    """
    Adam-style optimizer for pair variables using smoothed-max subgradients.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    m = int(x.size)
    n = 2 * m
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf"), x

    x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    b = None
    score = float("inf")

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = float("inf")

    m1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    v1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0
    beta1 = 0.75
    beta2 = 0.92
    eps = 1e-8

    lr = float(lr0)
    lr_min = 0.002
    lr_max = 0.25

    focus = float(focus_start)
    focus_inc = 1.007

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 900
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 28

    t0 = _now()
    it = 0

    while _now() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score = _score_from_arrays(a, b)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_score = score
            best_x = x.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0):
            print(f"[{label}] adam it={it} t={_now()-t0:.1f}s score={score:.6f} "
                  f"best={best_score:.6f} focus={focus:.2f} lr={lr:.4f} resets={resets}")

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.25)
            if rng.random() < 0.55:
                x = 0.70 * best_x + 0.30 * rng.normal(0.5, 0.32, size=m)
            else:
                x = best_x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.20, size=m)
            x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
            a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            m1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            v1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            step = 0
            lr = float(lr0)
            focus = float(rng.choice(np.array([10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 80.0, 160.0, 300.0])))
            continue

        p = _mixed_weights(b, focus, 0.25, 0.02)
        gx = _pair_gradient(p, a, n)
        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
        if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg < 1e-18:
            break

        gd = gx / maxg

        step += 1
        m1 = beta1 * m1 + (1.0 - beta1) * gd
        v1 = beta2 * v1 + (1.0 - beta2) * (gd * gd)

        b1 = 1.0 - beta1 ** step
        b2 = 1.0 - beta2 ** step
        mhat = m1 / b1
        vhat = v1 / b2
        update = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

        x_try = x - lr * update
        x_try = np.nan_to_num(x_try, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        x_try = np.clip(x_try, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

        if not np.any(x_try != x):
            x_try = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=m), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

        a_try = _pair_to_a(x_try, n)
        b_try = _fft_square(a_try)
        score_try = _score_from_arrays(a_try, b_try)

        wt_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        wt_new = float(np.dot(p, b_try))

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(score_try):
            if score_try < best_score - 1e-10:
                accept = True
            elif score_try < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif score_try < score * 1.003 and wt_new < wt_old * (1.0 - 1e-6):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            x = x_try
            a = a_try
            b = b_try
            score = score_try
            lr = min(lr * 1.03, lr_max)
        else:
            lr = max(lr * 0.80, lr_min)
            if lr < 0.005:
                x = np.clip(x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m), 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
                x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
                a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
                b = None
                score = float("inf")
                m1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                v1 = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                step = 0

        focus = min(focus * focus_inc, float(focus_max))
        it += 1

    best_a = _pair_to_a(best_x, n)
    return best_a, float(best_score), best_x


def _apply_flip_delta(b, a, j, n, delta, L):
    """Apply the exact convolution change caused by flipping one anti-pal pair."""
    i = int(j)
    r = int(n - 1 - i)
    d = float(delta)
    d2 = d * d

    si = max(0, i)
    ei = min(L, i + n)
    if si < ei:
        b[si:ei] += 2.0 * d * a[si - i: ei - i]

    sr = max(0, r)
    er = min(L, r + n)
    if sr < er:
        b[sr:er] -= 2.0 * d * a[sr - r: er - r]

    qi = 2 * i
    if 0 <= qi < L:
        b[qi] += d2

    qr = 2 * r
    if 0 <= qr < L:
        b[qr] += d2

    b[n - 1] -= 2.0 * d2


def _pair_binary_polish(x0, time_limit, rng, n, K=128, subset=256):
    """
    Stochastic local search over binary anti-palindromic orientations.
    Returns best binary x and its internal score.
    """
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    x = (x > 0.5).astype(np.float64)

    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    b = _fft_square(a)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxb)

    t = 1.0 / m
    L = 2 * n - 1

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 3000
    accepted = 0
    t0 = _now()
    it = 0

    while _now() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if K >= L - 1:
            Keff = L
            active = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
            outside_max = 0.0
        else:
            Keff = min(int(K), L - 1)
            active = np.argpartition(b, L - Keff)[-Keff:].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
            outside_max = float(np.partition(b, L - Keff - 1)[L - Keff - 1])

        S = min(int(subset), m)
        if S <= 0:
            break
        if S == m:
            idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            idx = rng.choice(m, size=S, replace=False).astype(np.int64)

        ii = idx[:, None]
        rr = n - 1 - ii
        ks = active[None, :]
        x_sub = x[idx]
        delta = t * (1.0 - 2.0 * x_sub)[:, None]

        idx1 = ks - ii
        idx2 = ks - rr

        mask1 = (idx1 >= 0) & (idx1 < n)
        mask2 = (idx2 >= 0) & (idx2 < n)

        a1 = np.where(mask1, a[np.clip(idx1, 0, n - 1)], 0.0)
        a2 = np.where(mask2, a[np.clip(idx2, 0, n - 1)], 0.0)

        q = (ks == 2 * ii).astype(np.float64)
        q += (ks == 2 * rr).astype(np.float64)
        q -= 2.0 * (ks == n - 1).astype(np.float64)

        db = 2.0 * delta * (a1 - a2) + (t * t) * q
        new_active = b[ks][None, :] + db
        est_max = np.maximum(outside_max, np.max(new_active, axis=1))

        jloc = int(np.argmin(est_max))
        j = int(idx[jloc])

        if est_max[jloc] < maxb - 1e-15 or no_improve > 900:
            old_x = float(x[j])
            old_ai = float(a[j])
            old_ar = float(a[n - 1 - j])
            b_old = b.copy()

            d = t * (1.0 - 2.0 * old_x)
            _apply_flip_delta(b, a, j, n, d, L)

            x[j] = 1.0 - old_x
            a[j] = old_ai + d
            a[n - 1 - j] = old_ar - d

            new_max = float(b.max())
            if new_max < maxb - 1e-15:
                maxb = new_max
                if 2.0 * n * maxb < best_score:
                    best_score = 2.0 * n * maxb
                    best_x = x.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                accepted += 1
                if accepted % 80 == 0:
                    b = _fft_square(a)
                    maxb = float(b.max())
                    if 2.0 * n * maxb < best_score:
                        best_score = 2.0 * n * maxb
                        best_x = x.copy()
            else:
                b = b_old
                x[j] = old_x
                a[j] = old_ai
                a[n - 1 - j] = old_ar
                no_improve += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > max_no:
            if rng.random() < 0.45:
                x = 0.5 + 0.5 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, m)
                x = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1.0)
                x = (x > 0.5).astype(np.float64)
            else:
                flip_count = max(1, m // 12)
                flip_idx = rng.choice(m, size=min(flip_count, m), replace=False)
                x[flip_idx] = 1.0 - x[flip_idx]

            a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
            b = _fft_square(a)
            maxb = float(b.max())
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.2)

        it += 1

    return best_x, float(best_score)


def _run_pair_kind(kind, n, x, time_limit, rng, label=""):
    """Run one pair-manifold optimization task."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if x.size != n // 2 or n <= 0 or x.size == 0:
        m = max(1, n // 2) if n > 0 else 0
        x = _random_pair_x(m, rng)
        n = 2 * x.size

    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

    if float(time_limit) <= 20.0:
        a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
        b = _fft_square(a)
        s = _score_from_arrays(a, b)
        return a, float(s), x

    if kind == "subgrad":
        fstart = 20.0 if n > 10000 else 10.0
        fmax = 450.0 if n > 10000 else 320.0
        return _optimize_pair_subgrad(x, time_limit, rng, label=label,
                                      focus_start=fstart, focus_max=fmax)
    if kind == "adam":
        fstart = 35.0 if n > 10000 else 25.0
        fmax = 500.0 if n > 10000 else 400.0
        return _optimize_pair_adam(x, time_limit, rng, label=label,
                                   focus_start=fstart, focus_max=fmax)
    if kind == "binary":
        x_out, sc = _pair_binary_polish(x, time_limit, rng, n)
        a_out = _pair_to_a(x_out, n)
        return a_out, float(sc), x_out

    # default fallback
    return _optimize_pair_subgrad(x, time_limit, rng, label=label)


def _optimize_full_subgrad(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False, label="",
                           focus_start=10.0, focus_max=300.0, eta0=0.25):
    """
    General nonnegative sequence optimizer, still using smoothed-max
    subgradients and multiplicative updates.
    """
    a = _clean_array(a0)
    if a.size < 2:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        a = a / s

    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a = a / float(a.sum())

    n = int(a.size)
    b = None
    score = float("inf")

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = float("inf")

    focus = float(focus_start)
    focus_inc = 1.013
    eta = float(eta0)
    eta_min = 0.01
    eta_max = 2.0

    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 1000
    resets = 0
    max_resets = 25

    t0 = _now()
    it = 0

    while _now() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        if b is None:
            b = _fft_square(a)
            score = _score_from_arrays(a, b)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-10:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0):
            print(f"[{label}] full it={it} t={_now()-t0:.1f}s score={score:.6f} "
                  f"best={best_score:.6f} focus={focus:.2f} eta={eta:.3f} resets={resets}")

        if no_improve > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > max_resets:
                break
            no_improve = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.25)

            if n % 2 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.6:
                x = _random_binary_x(n // 2, rng)
                a_pair = _pair_to_a(x, n)
                a = 0.75 * best_a + 0.25 * a_pair
            else:
                a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=n))

            a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss > 0.01:
                a /= ss
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()

            b = None
            score = float("inf")
            focus = float(rng.choice(np.array([5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0])))
            eta = 0.25
            continue

        p = _mixed_weights(b, focus, 0.25, 0.02)
        g = _fft_grad(p, a)
        mean_g = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gd = g - mean_g

        maxg = float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))
        if not np.isfinite(maxg) or maxg < 1e-18:
            break

        a_try = a * np.exp(-eta * (gd / maxg))
        a_try = np.nan_to_num(a_try, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a_try = np.maximum(a_try, 1e-300)
        ss = float(a_try.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-300:
            break
        a_try /= ss

        b_try = _fft_square(a_try)
        score_try = _score_from_arrays(a_try, b_try)

        wt_old = float(np.dot(p, b))
        wt_new = float(np.dot(p, b_try))

        accept = False
        if np.isfinite(score_try):
            if score_try < best_score - 1e-10:
                accept = True
            elif score_try < score - 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif score_try < score * 1.003 and wt_new < wt_old * (1.0 - 1e-6):
                accept = True

        if accept:
            a = a_try
            b = b_try
            score = score_try
            eta = min(eta * 1.05, eta_max)
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.75, eta_min)
            if eta < 0.05:
                a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, size=n))
                a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss > 0.01:
                    a /= ss
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                b = None
                score = float("inf")

        focus = min(focus * focus_inc, float(focus_max))
        it += 1

    return best_a, float(best_score)


def _consider_pair_x_update(x, n, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc):
    """
    Score a pair-variable candidate.  Updates overall best if it is better,
    and separately updates the best pair-manifold seed.
    """
    try:
        n = int(n)
    except Exception:
        return oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc

    if n <= 0 or n % 2 != 0:
        return oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = n // 2
    if x.size != m:
        x = _upsample_pair(x, m)

    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)

    a = _pair_to_a(x, n)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc

    b = _fft_square(a)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (s * s))

    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc

    if sc < osc:
        oa = a.copy()
        osc = float(sc)

    if sc < psc:
        px = x.copy()
        pn = int(n)
        pa = a.copy()
        psc = float(sc)

    return oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc


def _consider_full_update(a, oa, osc):
    """Score a full sequence and update overall best."""
    try:
        a = _clean_array(a)
        if a.size < 2:
            return oa, osc
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.01:
            return oa, osc
        a = a / s
        b = _fft_square(a)
        sc = float(2.0 * a.size * float(b.max()) / (s * s))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < osc:
            return a.copy(), float(sc)
        return oa, osc
    except Exception:
        return oa, osc


def _select_pair_seed(n, seed_kind, pair_x, pair_n, cache, rng):
    m = int(n) // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    if seed_kind is None or seed_kind == "best" or seed_kind == "pair_best":
        if pair_x is not None and np.asarray(pair_x).size > 0:
            return _upsample_pair(pair_x, m)
        return _random_pair_x(m, rng)

    if isinstance(cache, dict):
        key = str(seed_kind)
        if key in cache:
            val = np.asarray(cache[key], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if val.size == m:
                return val.copy()
            if val.size > 0:
                return _upsample_pair(val, m)

    return _random_pair_x(m, rng)


def _safe_evaluate(seq):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if ev is None or not callable(ev):
        return None
    try:
        return float(ev(seq))
    except Exception:
        return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point invoked by the harness.  Returns a list of nonnegative floats.
    """
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 2380.0

    if not np.isfinite(total) or total <= 60.0:
        total = 2380.0
    total = min(total, 2395.0)

    start = _now()
    final_deadline = start + total - 25.0

    rng_main = np.random.default_rng((seed * 1000003 + 12345) % (2**32))

    # Overall best (any nonnegative sequence) and best pair-manifold seed.
    oa = None
    osc = float("inf")
    px = None
    pn = 0
    pa = None
    psc = float("inf")

    # Read previously found construction, if available.
    try:
        given = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if given is not None:
            g = _clean_array(given)
            if g.size > 0:
                s = float(g.sum())
                if s > 0.01:
                    g = g / s
                    oa, osc = _consider_full_update(g, oa, osc)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if oa is None:
        oa = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        oa = oa / float(oa.sum())
        osc = 2.0

    # Initial pair seed from the current overall best, if it can be projected.
    if oa is not None:
        pjx = _project_pair_from_a(oa, oa.size)
        if pjx is not None:
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                pjx, oa.size, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )

    N1 = 8192
    N2 = 16384 if total >= 1600 else N1

    cache = {}
    quick_deadline = min(start + 130.0, final_deadline - 120.0)

    try:
        if _now() < quick_deadline:
            xg = _greedy_x(N1, rng_main, shuffle=True)
            cache["greedy1"] = xg
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                xg, N1, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        if _now() < quick_deadline:
            xg2 = _greedy_x(N1, rng_main, shuffle=False)
            cache["greedy1b"] = xg2
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                xg2, N1, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        if _now() < quick_deadline:
            xth = _thue_pair_x(N1 // 2, rng_main)
            cache["thue1"] = xth
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                xth, N1, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        if _now() < quick_deadline:
            xr = _random_binary_x(N1 // 2, rng_main)
            cache["randbin1"] = xr
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                xr, N1, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        if _now() < quick_deadline:
            xs = _good_sine_seed(N1, rng_main)
            cache["sine1"] = xs
            oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                xs, N1, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
            )
    except Exception:
        pass

    if N2 != N1:
        try:
            if _now() < quick_deadline:
                xth2 = _thue_pair_x(N2 // 2, rng_main)
                cache["thue2"] = xth2
                oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                    xth2, N2, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                )
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            if _now() < quick_deadline:
                xs2 = _good_sine_seed(N2, rng_main)
                cache["sine2"] = xs2
                oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                    xs2, N2, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                )
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"initial best: overall={osc:.6f} length={int(oa.size if oa is not None else 0)} "
          f"pair={psc:.6f} pairlen={pn}")

    tasks = []
    tasks.append(("binary", N1, "best", 90.0))
    tasks.append(("subgrad", N1, "best", 480.0))
    tasks.append(("subgrad", N1, "greedy1", 260.0))
    tasks.append(("adam", N1, "sine1", 260.0))
    if N2 != N1:
        tasks.append(("subgrad", N2, "best", 420.0))
        tasks.append(("adam", N2, "sine2", 300.0))
    tasks.append(("full", None, "best", 260.0))

    if total > 2000:
        reserve = 520.0
    else:
        reserve = max(90.0, 0.25 * total)
    task_deadline = start + total - max(reserve, 90.0) - 25.0

    for idx, (kind, n_task, seed_kind, max_alloc) in enumerate(tasks):
        now = _now()
        if now >= task_deadline:
            break
        alloc = min(float(max_alloc), task_deadline - now - 5.0)
        if alloc < 35.0:
            break

        task_seed = (seed * 1000003 + idx * 7919 + 17) % (2**32)
        task_rng = np.random.default_rng(task_seed)
        label = f"{kind}-n{n_task if n_task is not None else ''}-{task_seed % 10000}"
        print(f"task {idx} kind={kind} n={n_task} seed={seed_kind} alloc={alloc:.1f}s "
              f"overall={osc:.6f} pair={psc:.6f}")
        t0 = _now()

        try:
            if kind == "full":
                if oa is None:
                    continue

                if oa.size <= 12000:
                    a_seed = oa.copy()
                else:
                    n_full = N1
                    if px is not None and np.asarray(px).size > 0:
                        xf = _upsample_pair(px, n_full // 2)
                    else:
                        xf = _random_pair_x(n_full // 2, task_rng)
                    a_seed = _pair_to_a(xf, n_full)

                a_out, sc_out = _optimize_full_subgrad(
                    a_seed, alloc, task_rng,
                    verbose=False, label=label
                )
                oa, osc = _consider_full_update(a_out, oa, osc)

                # The projection of a full optimum may be a useful pair seed.
                if a_out is not None and a_out.size % 2 == 0:
                    pjx = _project_pair_from_a(a_out, a_out.size)
                    if pjx is not None:
                        oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                            pjx, a_out.size, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                        )
            else:
                n_use = int(n_task)
                x_seed = _select_pair_seed(n_use, seed_kind, px, pn, cache, task_rng)
                if x_seed.size == 0:
                    continue

                # Cheap pre-check/update.
                oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                    x_seed, n_use, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                )

                a_out, sc_out, x_out = _run_pair_kind(
                    kind, n_use, x_seed, alloc, task_rng, label=label
                )

                if a_out is not None and x_out is not None:
                    oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                        x_out, n_use, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                    )
        except Exception as exc:
            print("task failed:", repr(exc))

        print(f"task {idx} done used={_now()-t0:.1f}s overall={osc:.6f} pair={psc:.6f}")

    # Final polish.
    rem = final_deadline - _now()
    if rem > 70.0:
        use_pair = False
        if px is not None and np.asarray(px).size > 0:
            if oa is None:
                use_pair = True
            elif psc <= osc + 1e-7:
                use_pair = True
            elif pn == oa.size and abs(osc - psc) <= 1e-7:
                use_pair = True
            elif oa.size > 12000 and (osc - psc) < 0.02:
                use_pair = True

        rng_final1 = np.random.default_rng((seed + 99991) % (2**32))
        rng_final2 = np.random.default_rng((seed + 77777) % (2**32))

        if use_pair and px is not None and np.asarray(px).size > 0:
            n_final = int(pn) if (pn > 2 and pn % 2 == 0) else (oa.size if oa is not None and oa.size % 2 == 0 else N1)
            if n_final % 2 != 0 or n_final > 32768:
                n_final = N1

            x_final = _upsample_pair(px, n_final // 2)

            t1 = min(300.0, max(40.0, (rem - 45.0) * 0.55))
            try:
                a_out, sc_out, x_out = _run_pair_kind(
                    "subgrad", n_final, x_final, t1, rng_final1, label="final-sub"
                )
                if a_out is not None and x_out is not None:
                    oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                        x_out, n_final, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                    )
            except Exception as exc:
                print("final sub polish failed:", repr(exc))

            rem2 = final_deadline - _now()
            if rem2 > 70.0 and px is not None:
                x_final2 = _upsample_pair(px, n_final // 2)
                t2 = rem2 - 40.0
                try:
                    a_out, sc_out, x_out = _run_pair_kind(
                        "adam", n_final, x_final2, t2, rng_final2, label="final-adam"
                    )
                    if a_out is not None and x_out is not None:
                        oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc = _consider_pair_x_update(
                            x_out, n_final, oa, osc, px, pn, pa, psc
                        )
                except Exception as exc:
                    print("final adam polish failed:", repr(exc))
        else:
            if oa is not None and oa.size <= 20000:
                tfull = max(40.0, rem - 40.0)
                try:
                    a_out, sc_out = _optimize_full_subgrad(
                        oa, tfull, rng_final1, label="final-full"
                    )
                    oa, osc = _consider_full_update(a_out, oa, osc)
                except Exception as exc:
                    print("final full polish failed:", repr(exc))

    # Final safety and normalization.
    if oa is None:
        oa = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0

    oa = _clean_array(oa)
    ss = float(oa.sum())
    if ss <= 0.01:
        oa = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        ss = 1.0

    oa = oa / ss
    oa = np.nan_to_num(oa, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    oa = np.clip(oa, 0.0, 1000.0)

    final_seq = [float(v) for v in oa]

    # Optional exact self-check for moderate sizes (the official evaluator uses
    # direct convolution, so we do not rely on it for large returns).
    if oa.size <= 9000 and _now() < final_deadline - 4.0:
        exact = _safe_evaluate(final_seq)
        if exact is not None:
            print(f"final internal score={osc:.6f} exact score={exact:.6f} n={int(oa.size)}")
        else:
            print(f"final internal score={osc:.6f} exact unavailable n={int(oa.size)}")
    else:
        print(f"final internal score={osc:.6f} n={int(oa.size)}")

    return final_seq
```
